# YSH website

Cleaned text of the public YSH Property website. Each section is one scraped page. Photos of people, properties, and projects are kept. Logos, map tiles, placeholder avatars, and icon widgets are left out.

### About YSH Property

YSH is a dynamic property company that synergises its three arms – property development, property investment education and property management – to meet its vision of providing a holistic service to clients, where satisfaction is key.

The team brings together an immensity of knowledge and industry experience under the inspirational leadership of Dean Parker, who, with over 450 property transactions behind him, has built his company culture around progressive thinking, striving for excellence, and constantly ‘raising the bar’.

As property developers, YSH designs and delivers quality properties that are innovative and modern, highly functional, aesthetically beautiful, and healthy in terms of their low-emission construction materials and products.

As property investment educators, the team is passionate about assisting others to make informed and smart decisions around property investing through structured workshops as well as presentations at property forums and seminars.

As property managers, YSH offers ‘Satisfaction beyond the keys’ so that purchasers have immediate and ongoing support as they transition from buyers to occupiers or landlords, and well into the future, to ensure the maintenance and continued success of their asset.

For the client who sets out to learn about property investment, purchase a home, buy an investment property, build a property portfolio or all of the above, the team prides itself on ensuring that the process is as smooth and stress-free as possible, and that every aspect is delivered to an exacting and exemplary professional standard where chance and luck play no part.

Property Management

Published by [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/) on 2025-04-08T17:02:54+10:00

### Property Management

### The YSH Difference

Built on more than 18 years’ property industry experience, YSH now offers Property Management services to our valued clients. We focus on providing a stress-free experience by taking care of all aspects of investment property ownership.

Our objective is to deliver a high quality property, and through exceptional management services ensure it remains a strong investment for many years to come.

Our specialist Property Management team is focused on finding you a qualified tenant in the fastest possible time. We are here to provide you with a stress-free experience while you grow your investment portfolio. With regular market research updates, industry knowledge and progressive thinking, YSH offers a unique property management experience.

### Available for Rent

[CLICK HERE – Available Properties to Rent](https://app.inspectrealestate.com.au/External/ROL/QuickWeb.aspx?AgentAccountName=YSH&type=rental&hideprice=FALSE&iframe=false&hidelogo=)

### Our Services

### Financial Accounting and Reporting

We provide you with timely and accurate accounting and reporting, saving you time and money when dealing with your accountant. We disburse your funds in a timely manner with mid and end of month disbursements.

### Collection of Rent

We have a zero arrears policy. Handing over the responsibility of rent collection to us eliminates one of your greatest concerns, providing you with peace of mind.

### Inspections

YSH understands the importance of routine inspections. Commencing shortly after your new tenant moves in, our timely scheduled inspections and detailed inspection reports will ensure that your property remains well looked after and obtains the maximum rental income.

### Tenant Selection

All tenants are screened thoroughly. We ensure no step is missed and provide transparent advice regarding any application received. All applications are discussed with you, the owner to seek final approval.

### Marketing to Maximise Your Rental Return

By using the latest technology and marketing tools, we expose your property to the widest range of prospective tenants. We stay up to date with market trends and rental incomes for your property type and use a combination of internet advertising, signage and comprehensive data base searches to secure tenants on your behalf. Our Property Management division will actively market your property to potential tenants while evaluating market conditions, searching comparable property rents and evaluating vacancy factors.

### Trust

YSH offers more than 12 years’ experience in Property Development and Investment and offers a broad knowledge of the Brisbane suburbs. This combined with 15 years’ experience in Property Management resulting in the provision of unique and tailored services that have been developed with your needs fore-most in mind. We pride ourselves on offering a highly regarded and professional service in an industry that at times, can be challenging to navigate.

### The YSH Difference

Finding somewhere to live shouldn’t feel like a second job. With more than 25 years in the property industry, we’ve built YSH around one idea: leasing should be fast, honest, and stress-free.

Every property listed here is ready to view, straightforward to apply for, and backed by a team that actually answers the phone. No chasing, no guessing games.

### Frequently Asked Questions

How do I book an inspection?

Find your property in the listings above and click Book Inspection. You will be able to choose a time that works for you.

What do I need to apply?

Photo ID, proof of income, and rental history if you have it. The online application walks you through each step.

How do I access my property on move-in day?

Moving in is fast and simple. Every property uses secure keyless entry instead of traditional keys. On your move-in day, we will email you a unique digital access code. Just arrive, enter your code, and you are home, no waiting around for keys.

Are utilities included?

Yes. Unless otherwise stated in your lease agreement, your weekly price includes electricity, water and high-speed NBN.

Is the internet included?

Yes, high-speed NBN is included in every property. Your wifi details are shared on move-in day, and you can also find them anytime through your tenant portal via the QR code at your door.

Can I bring my own furniture?

Every property comes fully furnished for comfort and practicality. Due to space and safety, large items like beds can't be swapped or removed. Want to bring a small piece, like a shelf or side table? Email us a photo and measurements and we'll check with the property owner.

Can I drop off my belongings before my lease starts?

We understand moving is a busy time, but for insurance reasons you can only access the property from your official lease start date. Rest assured, everything will be ready for you when you arrive.

What if something needs fixing?

All maintenance requests go through our online system, Tapi. Scan the QR code in your kitchen with your phone and follow the prompts to report any issue quickly, our dedicated maintenance team takes it from there.

Can I bring a pet?

Due to the shared nature of our properties, pets are not permitted. This keeps things comfortable and fair for everyone living on site.

How does bond work?

Your bond is typically four weeks' rent, held securely for the length of your lease and refunded when you move out, provided the property is left in good condition.

Still have questions? Reach out to our team, we recommend calling for the fastest response.

### About YSH Property

YSH is a dynamic property company that synergises its three arms – property development, property investment education and property management – to meet its vision of providing a holistic service to clients, where satisfaction is key.

The team brings together an immensity of knowledge and industry experience under the inspirational leadership of Dean Parker, who, with over 450 property transactions behind him, has built his company culture around progressive thinking, striving for excellence, and constantly ‘raising the bar’.

As property developers, YSH designs and delivers quality properties that are innovative and modern, highly functional, aesthetically beautiful, and healthy in terms of their low-emission construction materials and products.

As property investment educators, the team is passionate about assisting others to make informed and smart decisions around property investing through structured workshops as well as presentations at property forums and seminars.

As property managers, YSH offers ‘Satisfaction beyond the keys’ so that purchasers have immediate and ongoing support as they transition from buyers to occupiers or landlords, and well into the future, to ensure the maintenance and continued success of their asset.

For the client who sets out to learn about property investment, purchase a home, buy an investment property, build a property portfolio or all of the above, the team prides itself on ensuring that the process is as smooth and stress-free as possible and that every aspect is delivered to an exacting and exemplary professional standard where chance and luck played no part.

### About YSH Property

YSH Property was built under the leadership of Dean Parker, whose experience across hundreds of property transactions shaped a company culture centred on progressive thinking and constantly raising the bar.

Today, that same standard shows up in every micro apartment we manage across Queensland and Victoria. Every property, whether purpose-built or thoughtfully adapted, is designed around one principle: your own private space. Quality fixtures, modern layouts, and healthier living environments, all part of how we think properties should work for the people living in them.

Our team manages each property directly, so when something needs fixing or a question needs answering, you’re talking to the people who actually know your home. We handle the everyday details so leasing feels simple: fast responses, clear communication, and support that doesn’t stop once you’ve signed.

## Privacy Policy

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/privacy-policy/](https://ysh.com.au/privacy-policy/)

Privacy Policy

Published by [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/) on 2025-12-17T10:17:37+10:00

This Privacy Policy governs the manner in which YSH Property collects, uses, maintains and discloses information collected from users (each, a “User”) of the [YSH Property](https://ysh.com.au/privacy-policy/) website (“Site”).

### Personal identification information

We may collect personal identification information from Users in a variety of ways, including, but not limited to, when Users visit our site, place an order, fill out a form, and in connection with other activities, services, features or resources we make available on our Site.Users may be asked for, as appropriate, name, email address, phone number. Users may, however, visit our Site anonymously. We will collect personal identification information from Users only if they voluntarily submit such information to us. Users can always refuse to supply personally identification information, except that it may prevent them from engaging in certain Site related activities.

#### Non-personal identification information

We may collect non-personal identification information about Users whenever they interact with our Site. Non-personal identification information may include the browser name, the type of computer and technical information about Users means of connection to our Site, such as the operating system and the Internet service providers utilized and other similar information.

##### Web browser cookies

Our Site may use “cookies” to enhance User experience. User’s web browser places cookies on their hard drive for record-keeping purposes and sometimes to track information about them. User may choose to set their web browser to refuse cookies, or to alert you when cookies are being sent. If they do so, note that some parts of the Site may not function properly.

##### How we use collected information

YSH Property may collect and use Users personal information for the following purposes:

- *To run and operate our Site* We may need your information display content on the Site correctly.
- *To improve customer service* Information you provide helps us respond to your customer service requests and support needs more efficiently.

##### How we protect your information

We adopt appropriate data collection, storage and processing practices and security measures to protect against unauthorized access, alteration, disclosure or destruction of your personal information, username, password, transaction information and data stored on our Site.

##### Sharing your personal information

We do not sell, trade, or rent Users personal identification information to others. We may share generic aggregated demographic information not linked to any personal identification information regarding visitors and users with our business partners, trusted affiliates and advertisers for the purposes outlined above.

##### Third party websites

Users may find advertising or other content on our Site that link to the sites and services of our partners, suppliers, advertisers, sponsors, licencors and other third parties. We do not control the content or links that appear on these sites and are not responsible for the practices employed by websites linked to or from our Site. In addition, these sites or services, including their content and links, may be constantly changing. These sites and services may have their own privacy policies and customer service policies. Browsing and interaction on any other website, including websites which have a link to our Site, is subject to that website’s own terms and policies.

##### Changes to this privacy policy

YSH Property has the discretion to update this privacy policy at any time. When we do, we will revise the updated date at the bottom of this page. We encourage Users to frequently check this page for any changes to stay informed about how we are helping to protect the personal information we collect. You acknowledge and agree that it is your responsibility to review this privacy policy periodically and become aware of modifications.

##### Your acceptance of these terms

By using this Site, you signify your acceptance of this policy. If you do not agree to this policy, please do not use our Site. Your continued use of the Site following the posting of changes to this policy will be deemed your acceptance of those changes.

##### Contacting us

If you have any questions about this Privacy Policy, the practices of this site, or your dealings with this site, please contact us.

## Healthy Homes

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/healthy-homes/](https://ysh.com.au/healthy-homes/)

Healthy Homes

Published by [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/) on 2021-04-30T14:45:50+10:00

### The Healthy Homes Story

At YSH we are passionate about health and are proactive about delivering healthy homes to our clients. We went through the heart-breaking experience of losing Elise’s father to prostate cancer and caring for him while he deteriorated from a fit 59-year-old who’d always maintained a healthy lifestyle to his sad passing away just two and a half years later. This personal tragedy set us on a course of intense learning about environmental chemical exposure and its effect on our health.

We know that we can play sport, work-out at the gym, eat well, get plenty of sleep, cover up in the sun, choose cosmetic products wisely, avoid outdoor pollutants and buy environmentally friendly household products, but all efforts to live well and stay healthy can be effectively negated if we don’t have a healthy home. Home is, after all, where we spend most of our time. We also know that primary prevention is the simplest and most economical way to address potential environmental health risks in our homes.

For us, this means designing our homes around our clients’ wellbeing and selecting the healthier alternatives wherever possible when it comes to the products and materials we use in construction and fit-out.

We recognise that hazards can’t be avoided completely but a lot can be done to minimise risk and ensure the safest use of the space. From garage placement and ventilation, to the positioning of electrical wiring and appliances and the use of integrated screening methods to reduce occupants’ exposure to EMF radiation, our designs reflect the value we place on health and wellbeing.

![data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%271100%27%20height%3D%27733%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%201100%20733%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%271100%27%20height%3D%27733%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E)

In terms of the emissions, the main offenders fall within the broad group of carbon-containing chemicals referred to as volatile organic compounds (VOCs), which included known carcinogens such as formaldehyde, methylene chloride, toluene and xylene. These compounds ‘volatilise’, or transform to a vapour, at room temperature. From building frames, to timbers, wall linings, insulation, kitchen cabinetry (especially MDF and particle boards, and laminates), doors, flooring materials and drapes, we strive to select products with low VOC ratings or zero-emission (E0) ratings wherever possible. Similarly, products such as paints, coatings, sealants, groutings, adhesives and water-proofing agents contain high levels of VOCs and our choice is low-emission products.

The effects on health can include respiratory issues, irritations of the eyes and throat, headaches, dizziness, liver and kidney damage, and central nervous system damage. Further, a significant proportion of cancers are known to be caused by environmental toxins. And poor quality residential air can also exacerbate other illnesses. Particularly susceptible to adverse health effects are vulnerable people such as the elderly, infants, pregnant women and those who are immunocompromised. For children exposed to formaldehyde, an increased risk of asthma is probable, and allergic sensitisation to common allergens is proven.

The level of exposure to environmental health hazards in the home is, to a large extent, a choice. At YSH we believe the value of health cannot be over-rated, and we are proud of our choice to create and deliver healthy homes. We know that many construction materials contain compounds that are harmful, some even carcinogenic, to humans, and that these are emitted slowly over many years as the materials off-gas into the environment. And if that environment is a poorly ventilated enclosed space such as a house, occupants are continually exposed to those harmful emissions, with both short-term and long-term adverse health effects.

![data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%27800%27%20height%3D%27533%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%20800%20533%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%27800%27%20height%3D%27533%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E)

About YSH Property

Published by [marcos](https://ysh.com.au/author/marcos/) on 2026-09-24T14:49:04+10:00

### About YSH Property

YSH Property was built under the leadership of Dean Parker, whose experience across hundreds of property transactions shaped a company culture centred on progressive thinking and constantly raising the bar.

Today, that same standard shows up in every micro apartment we manage across Queensland and Victoria. Every property, whether purpose-built or thoughtfully adapted, is designed around one principle: your own private space. Quality fixtures, modern layouts, and healthier living environments, all part of how we think properties should work for the people living in them.

Our team manages each property directly, so when something needs fixing or a question needs answering, you’re talking to the people who actually know your home. We handle the everyday details so leasing feels simple: fast responses, clear communication, and support that doesn’t stop once you’ve signed.

### Our History

YSH exists to do one thing well: create homes people actually want to live in, and keep them running smoothly long after moving day. For over 25 years, under Dean Parker's leadership, the team has built a culture around one idea, raising the bar, every time, backed by real data from managing over 1,000 properties and counting.

Every YSH property is purpose-built for one thing: private, independent living that doesn't cost like a full-sized apartment. Modern and genuinely functional, designed around how people actually live day to day, not retrofitted, not an afterthought. It doesn't stop at the keys. From your first day to your last, the team stays involved, because a good home shouldn't need luck to stay that way.

### Mission

Satisfaction Beyond The Keys.

### Vision

To be an industry leader in functional design and creating liveable, healthy homes.

### Values

#### Determined

Hardworking and persistent, we always follow up and follow through.

#### Systematic

Accurate and thorough, we create and respect the process.

#### Informed

A thirst for learning and continuous improvement, we share what we know.

#### Connected

Building strong, respectful relationships, we stand by our product.

### The Team Behind Every Move-In

The team behind YSH brings genuine energy and commitment to every apartment they manage, being proactive, hands-on, and quick to sort things out rather than pass them along. That same commitment shows up in the numbers: more than 25 years of experience, and over 1,000 micro apartments currently under care.

[Meet The Team](https://ysh.com.au/team-2/)

About Micro Apartments

Published by [marcos](https://ysh.com.au/author/marcos/) on 2026-09-24T14:48:15+10:00

### About Micro Apartments

A YSH micro apartment is a brand new, thoughtfully designed home – fully furnished, self-contained, and entirely your own. A separate bedroom, living area, bathroom and kitchenette, with electricity, water and internet already included in one weekly payment.

Move in without moving boxes. No furniture to buy, no bills to set up, no weekend lost assembling a flat pack. Everything's ready before you arrive, so you spend your first week living, not unpacking.

A real address, without the price tag. Queensland and Victoria's best locations, without the cost of a much bigger apartment to match. Same city, same convenience, a fraction of the commitment.

## Green Healthy Design

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_10/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_10/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_12/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/ysh-property-leads-the-market-because-leadership-is-more-than-just-numbers/)

- [View Larger Image![Green Healthy Design1](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/Green-Healthy-Design1.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/Green-Healthy-Design1.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), August 24th, 2018. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

The Garden Terraces, the latest project from developer Your Style Group, has an excellent location offering access to a wealth of retail, leisure and transport facilities.

Two cottages on Garden Tce in Newmarket mark the location of the suburbs newest apartment project. The 1417sq m developments site was acquired from Marquette Properties for just over $2 million by Your Style Group. With Approval in place for 26 apartments, and construction expected to start by April.

Your Style Group managing director Dean parker said his organisation would continue its approach of building high-quality apartments to attract buyers. He said high-end finishes created a home two steps ahead of most entry-level units being marketed at present.

“For every particular suburb we deal with, we try to produce the best possible product for that area,” Mr Parker said.

“If we can design a really good product for Newmarket that means an owner occupier would love living there and a tenant would love living there – then from an investor’s point of view, that means they’ll have a long-term tenant because we’ve designed a great building that looks good and works well.”

Close to transport and shopping, buyers can choose between one, two and three-bedroom units priced from the low-$400,000s to the mid-$600,000s.

Mr Parker said residents could enjoy more outdoor living through a dual-building layout.

“There’s a very nice open green space in between the two buildings, so instead of having an internal foyer like most apartment buildings, we’ll have this open air space with all the greenery,” he said. Mr Parker said benefits included great cross ventilation and healthy common areas for occupants to enjoy.

But the resident-focused design doesn’t end there, with Your Style Group using low-emission paints, carpets, sealants and glues to protect occupants’ health. “If you walk into of our products you generally won’t get that new house smell because we’ve chosen products that don’t off-gas,” Mr Parker said.

Build completion is set for early 2018.

## Boutique Developer Creates Secret Sales Weapon

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_12/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_12/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_11/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_10/)

- [View Larger Image![Boutique Developer Creates Secret Sales Weapon](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/Boutique-Developer-Creates-Secret-Sales-Weapon.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/Boutique-Developer-Creates-Secret-Sales-Weapon.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), May 5th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

Brisbane developer Your Style Homes has created a transportable sales office out of a shipping container, enabling the boutique developer to compete with million-dollar sales displays of large-scale residential projects, at a fraction of the cost.

Your Style Homes’ modern 20sqm sales pod features a thick steel exterior and heavy duty doors and can be instantly shifted to service any of its current developments.

After several months of planning, the self-contained capsule now includes full working scaled project models, finishes boards, artistic renders and a fully interactive digital display plus air conditioning and storage.

Your Style Homes Managing Director Dean Parker said the flexible and interactive office model creates a “level playing field” for smaller developers and provides a flexible base for each of its off the plan projects.

“The sales capsule is waterproof, weatherproof, and with the build and internal fitout co-ordinated by Your Style Homes’ in-house team development team– the result is a high quality sales and information centre which contains everything buyers need when considering a property purchase.

“It’s a great solution for boutique developers who service Brisbane’s inner-city ring, where a sales centre is required for several projects, but million dollar sales offices for each project are neither feasible nor practical.

“The capsule delivers an interactive space that provides commercial functionality and captures each project’s vision,” he said. “It’s the modern approach to presenting property,” he said.

“This capsule connects to the land lot’s main power source, uses very little electricity and provides a cost-efficient, flexible way to compete with bigger projects,” he said.

Mr Parker said buyers had been impressed with the sales capsule’s design and presentation.

“Buyers come through and say, ‘is this a shipping container?’ and it provokes conversation about the benefits of recycling. Buyers are impressed with the modern approach,” he said.

Your Style Homes has two project currently selling the tightly-held Newmarket area, which was recently identified as Brisbane’s fastest selling suburb, by leading market research firm, Place Advisory.

These will be Newmaket’s first new apartment developments in suburb for over three years.

Your Style Homes’ Eve on Erneton project is 50 per cent sold with only 6 remaining, with two and three bedroom apartments starting from $480,000. Eve is on track for completion mid-2017.

Just a few streets away, its Garden Terraces project is nearly 40% sold with 16 apartments remaining with two and three bedroom apartments from $465,000. Garden Terraces is scheduled to commence construction prior to mid-2017.

Your Style Homes Sales and Information Centre is located at its Garden Terrace site, at 50 Garden Terrace, Newmarket. Call to book an appointment.

## Rare opportunities in Brisbane’s fastest selling suburb

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_11/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_11/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_9/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_12/)

- [View Larger Image![fastest selling brisbane](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/fastest-selling-brisbane.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/fastest-selling-brisbane.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), April 13th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

Newmarket was recently identified by leading market research firm, Place Advisory, as the fastest selling suburb for apartments in Brisbane’s inner ring.

The popularity of Newmarket is driven by its ideal position – just 5kms from the CBD, well connected by train and bus services, surrounded by an abundance of lifestyle attractions and amenities, and flaunting an extensive selection of dining and entertaining venues.

Boutique Queensland developer, Your Style Homes has now doubled its residential footprint in Newmarket, with the new 26 apartment development, The Garden Terraces.

Eve and Garden Terraces will be Newmarket’s first new apartment developments in over three years, with the last previous apartment development being completed in late 2014.

[YSH Property Leads the Market | Because Leadership Is More Than Just Numbers](https://ysh.com.au/ysh-property-leads-the-market-because-leadership-is-more-than-just-numbers/)

## COMING SOON: A New Level of Luxury Living in Stafford

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_7/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_7/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_6/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_8/)

- [View Larger Image![COMING SOON A New Level of Luxury Living in Stafford](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2017/02/COMING-SOON-A-New-Level-of-Luxury-Living-in-Stafford.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2017/02/COMING-SOON-A-New-Level-of-Luxury-Living-in-Stafford.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), February 27th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

This latest Your Style Homes development has been designed as a sophisticated, serene boutique townhouse enclave designed to offer premium near-city living, less than 10 kilometres from Brisbane’s CBD on Gamelin Crescent, in one of Brisbane’s most desired suburbs.

Incorporating eight architecturally-designed luxury townhomes located less than 15 minutes by car from Brisbane’s CBD, each three-bedroom private residence combines the perfect blend of indoor and outdoor living space, a hallmark of well-designed modern homes.

Architecturally designed to be light-filled and spacious for owner-occupier living or a savvy townhouse investment, the residences’ potential is underpinned by their close proximity to shops including Stafford City and a short drive to Brisbane Airport.

Managing Director, Dean Parker said: “Stafford has long held a reputation of value for money, stability and desirability.”

“Located near some of Brisbane’s best parklands, and with abundant amenity and transport, Stafford is a key area of urban renewal and will always achieve exceptional buyer interest due to its proximity to Brisbane’s CBD.

“This leafy location is strengthened by some of Queensland’s best transport infrastructure including both the Airport Link and Clem7 Tunnels, and features a family and community- focused culture. Stafford is a suburb which will only grow from here,” he said.

“We’re excited to be able to present such an exceptional development to this suburb.”

Construction will commence in early 2017.

## A Buyers’ Guide to Purchasing Apartments

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_9/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_9/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_8/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_11/)

- [View Larger Image![A Buyers Guide to Purchasing Apartments](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/A-Buyers-Guide-to-Purchasing-Apartments.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/08/A-Buyers-Guide-to-Purchasing-Apartments.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), March 13th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

### How To Invest Wisely

When it comes to buying an apartment, smart investors know that it takes a variety of factors to pinpoint and secure a good investment.

Most experts predict that while current interest rates are low, Brisbane property offers a ‘standout’ rating when looking to grow your investment portfolio.

Below, we discuss three major considerations for apartment buyers.

#### 1\. The Location

It is important to invest in middle-ring locations, generally no further out than 10 kilometres from Brisbane’ GPO. But no matter where you invest, staying close to major transport links in the order of rail, major roads, and busways ensures that residents can easily access amenity.

Residents generally seek a 5-10-minute commute to major employment nodes including hospitals, universities and major retail opportunities.

Green spaces including parks and recreation, plus destination and lifestyle facilitates are desirable within close proximity. Excellent landscaping creates a ‘finished’ aesthetic that residents love.

##### 2\. The Development

Smaller, boutique developments are favoured by residents for their personalised, high quality and detailed approach to living. Less than 50 units within a complex means that residents aren’t crowded or overwhelmed and can maintain a greater sense of security.

Multiple landscaped nodes throughout the complex maintains the high quality appeal of the development and gives residents spaces to relax.

###### 3\. The Design

A bespoke, high quality offering combined with secure undercover parking is integral for a well-designed apartment.

Inside, a gallery kitchen with bench space to seat a group of people, a separate dining area, and excellent natural light and ventilation ensures maximum comfort. Covered patio space, with enough to seat four, complemented with open space will enhance year-round liveability.

A master bedroom including ample robe space, spacious ensuite, with a separate laundry and storage will ensure liveability through separation of spaces.

Air conditioning in living areas, ceiling fans in bedrooms and on patio, and clever maximisation of spaces means residents have everything required without being confined.

And of course… The higher the ceilings, the better!

## Construction Begins at Eve on Erneton

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_6/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_6/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_5/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_7/)

- [View Larger Image![Construction Begins at Eve on Erneton](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Construction-Begins-at-Eve-on-Erneton.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Construction-Begins-at-Eve-on-Erneton.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), February 20th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

Construction has commenced on Your Style Homes’ $6 million multi-residential project Eve on Erneton in inner-city Newmarket.

The 812 sqm site at 53 Erneton Street, Newmarket, will feature 12 two and three-bedroom single-level contemporary apartments across three storeys, 5 kilometres from Brisbane’s CBD.

Your Style Homes Managing Director, Dean Parker said the site’s 20-metre frontage to Erneton Street represented a rare opportunity in the leafy, low-density pocket where Developmental Approvals are rarely granted.

“Newmarket is a tightly held suburb with a lack of developmental opportunity, so we are confident in our decision to announce the construction of Eve; we know we had been granted a special opportunity by securing this elevated site.

“It’s the only project of its type underway in the area, and I believe our proposal received approval due to the increasing demand for apartments just outside the CBD, offeringlarger residences and high quality finishes.”

“Eve on Erneton apartments have been designed as boutique, high-quality luxury dwellings, designed to appeal to the owner-occupier market which has expressed strong demand to live in the area. However, with a previous shortage of apartment stock in Newmarket, these buyers have in the past turned to Kelvin Grove, Wilston or Lutwyche.

“Eve’s apartment prices start at $480,000 and offer functionality, great lifestyle and affordability to first-home buyers, who are able to take advantage of the $20,000 Queensland First Home Owner Grant.

“We’ve received strong interest in this stock, even in these early stages,” Mr. Parker said. Mr Parker said Eve on Erneton will embrace the character and charm of Newmarket.

Completion is expected in May 2017.

## Staff Insight: Nick Crang – Development Manager

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_5/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_5/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_4/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_6/)

- [View Larger Image![Staff Insight Nick Crang Development Manager](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Staff-Insight-Nick-Crang-Development-Manager.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Staff-Insight-Nick-Crang-Development-Manager.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), February 13th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

Design and Functionality are of fundamental importance to good development. Unfortunately ‘good development’ has no shortcuts.

I often say that If I do my job well most people will never notice and I firmly believe this to be true.

Creating successful properties for our clients requires a rather paradoxical blend of bland, meticulous process, and a constant slightly wacky questioning of ‘what is’.

Personally, I fear the innate practices that many developers proudly hang their hat upon. If left unchecked it can only lead to one of two things, obsolete substandard ill-fitting product or unhappy clients and we do not strive for either.

It is true, the pragmatic process of “delivering” a home for our clients is the same or very similar from one project to the next however; the context of who what and why is never the same and varies greatly. This is the reason that the design intent should never be assumed or taken for granted.

Every completed development design is the sum of an inordinate amount of consideration and attention to detail in order to deliver on our commitment to function and design. We take pride in this process, it is my duty and our commitment as a company.

## Queensland Architectural History Reimagined for Today

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_1/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_1/)

[Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_2/)

- [View Larger Image![Queensland Architectural History Reimagined for Today](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Queensland-Architectural-History-Reimagined-for-Today.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Queensland-Architectural-History-Reimagined-for-Today.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), January 16th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

A beautifully restored century-old Queensland homestead on one of Coorparoo’s most prestigious streets is the centrepiece of Your Style Homes’ new $5 million inner-city residential development, Aspect Coorparoo.

The original Queenslander on the site has been expertly reimagined and reconfigured into three spacious, three bedroom townhouses.

These premium residences are complemented by the strikingarchitectural profile of an additional five, new three- bedroom townhouses, creating a total of eight residences of real character.

The project’s elevated position on the apex of a hill at 43 Raby Road, Coorparoo, means residents will enjoy full views of Brisbane’s city skyline. Seven of eight townhomes in the project have now sold with just one opportunity left to be part of this amazing lifestyle.

Your Style Homes Managing Director Dean Parker explained that the development aimed to carefully restore the site’s existing home and incorporate the modern additions to the side and rear in line with the look and feel of the street.

“The biggest challenge of the project has been maintaining the existing home, refurbishing it to the highest standards while seamlessly integrating new, modern dwellings,” Mr Parker said.

“Original homes like this were built to suit a British style of living with small windows and small rooms, so we weaved in the modern style of development living – with large, open, light-filled spaces – while maintaining the essence of the existing house.

“We retained and refurbished the existing front façade of the Queenslander fronting

Raby Road to enhance the relationship and preserve the character and splendour using the original roof lines to enhance its classic street appeal,” he said.

Mr Parker noted that rebuilding the home piece-by-piece to reach current building standards required careful planning.

“It was important for us to strike an appropriate contextual balance that paid homage to the past within a contemporary feel. We are very proud of the way it has come together and that has been evidenced by the great response from buyers.”

Finishes throughout the project have been sourced to match the quality of the design with the stone benchtops, timber flooring, Roger Seller kitchen and bathroom fittings and premium-grade appliances buyers would expect from a modern inner-city development.

Only one townhouse remains for sale in the project and includes three bedrooms, 2.5 bathrooms and two car paces. Townhouse 8 is available at $719,950.

## A Message from Managing Director Dean Parker

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_3/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_3/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_2/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_4/)

- [View Larger Image![A Message from Managing Director Dean Parker](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/A-Message-from-Managing-Director-Dean-Parker.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/A-Message-from-Managing-Director-Dean-Parker.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), January 30th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

It’s my great pleasure to welcome you to Your Style Homes’ Summer Update 2017.

While it is a great time to be living in Queensland, it is also a great time to be owning property in Brisbane. Corelogic recently reported that the Brisbane-Gold Coast corridor’s median property values (houses and apartments) rose 5.7 per cent in the 12 months to the end of June 2016, compared to 3.5 per cent growth in the previous 12 months.

As a family-owned, boutique residential development company, our people are at the heart of everything we do. Our family-centric approach to property creates a warm and dedicated environment that attracts a talented and experienced team of employees with extensive experience in the industry.

We invite you to learn more about Your Style Homes as we explore our exciting new development releases across inner Brisbane with current residential offerings positioned in Brisbane’s best inner-city suburbs including Coorparoo, Ascot, and Newmarket.

Also included are some of our market success stories including Allure on Ludwick – our Cannon Hill development – and our upcoming releases for 2017.

Your Style Homes is focused on creating multi-faceted lifestyle addresses that raise the benchmark for inner-city living. Having a customer-centric approach to our business means we’re able to gather the needs and wants of our clients and integrate these in a functional, beautiful design that delivers luxurious and quality-rich homes in the best inner- city locations.

By understanding our city and the ways people want to live, we create beautiful residences that make people happy.

### Frequently Asked Questions

How do I book an inspection?

Find your property in the listings above and click Book Inspection. You will be able to choose a time that works for you.

What do I need to apply?

Photo ID, proof of income, and rental history if you have it. The online application walks you through each step.

How do I access my property on move-in day?

Moving in is fast and simple. Every property uses secure keyless entry instead of traditional keys. On your move-in day, we will email you a unique digital access code. Just arrive, enter your code, and you are home, no waiting around for keys.

Are utilities included?

Yes. Unless otherwise stated in your lease agreement, your weekly price includes electricity, water and high-speed NBN.

Is the internet included?

Yes, high-speed NBN is included in every property. Your wifi details are shared on move-in day, and you can also find them anytime through your tenant portal via the QR code at your door.

Can I bring my own furniture?

Every property comes fully furnished for comfort and practicality. Due to space and safety, large items like beds can't be swapped or removed. Want to bring a small piece, like a shelf or side table? Email us a photo and measurements and we'll check with the property owner.

Can I drop off my belongings before my lease starts?

We understand moving is a busy time, but for insurance reasons you can only access the property from your official lease start date. Rest assured, everything will be ready for you when you arrive.

What if something needs fixing?

All maintenance requests go through our online system, Tapi. Scan the QR code in your kitchen with your phone and follow the prompts to report any issue quickly, our dedicated maintenance team takes it from there.

Can I bring a pet?

Due to the shared nature of our properties, pets are not permitted. This keeps things comfortable and fair for everyone living on site.

How does bond work?

Your bond is typically four weeks' rent, held securely for the length of your lease and refunded when you move out, provided the property is left in good condition.

Still have questions? Reach out to our team, we recommend calling for the fastest response.

## Simone Finds Her Style at Allure Cannon Hill

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_2/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_2/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_1/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_3/)

- [View Larger Image![Simone Finds Her Style at Allure Cannon Hill](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Simone-Finds-Her-Style-at-Allure-Cannon-Hill.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Simone-Finds-Her-Style-at-Allure-Cannon-Hill.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), January 23rd, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

When busy first homebuyer, Simone Fernandes, arrived in Brisbane from New Zealand recently, she knew she wanted to purchase a private, secure apartment – but most importantly, it had to be on the fast track to completion.

“I knew I was looking to move into somewhere fast. It had to be new, beautiful but also something that would be completed soon. I didn’t want to wait around for years for something to be constructed,” the 21-year old said.

Enter Your Style Homes’ recently sold out ‘Allure on Ludwick’ development at 58 Ludwick Street, Cannon Hill.

“I was drawn to Allure because it was tucked away and removed from the noise of a main road; it had boutique charm, nestled amongst trees with lots of natural light owing in, with an open oor plan, modern design and good security,” Ms Fernandes said.

We recognised the potential for smaller boutique developments in the area, which has 100 per cent sold out with renters now moving in.

Ms Fernandes said the low maintenance living was perfect for her busy lifestyle.

“As someone new to the country, I wanted the privacy that a smaller boutique development offered, with all the added benefits such as low maintenance living, high quality space and a beautiful balcony, but without the large volumes of residents,” Ms Fernandes said.

Ms. Fernandes’ job as an airline cabin crew member required her to start at varying hours of the day, so security was crucial.

“Sometimes I’m up very early for work, so I needed to have a very secure basement and car parking area, which Allure had,” she said. “What Allure represented was unbeatable.”

Your Style Homes Managing, Director Dean Parker, said Allure had been popular with investors due to Cannon Hill’s low vacancy rate of just 1.22 percent.

“One bedroom apartments at Allure started at $330,000 – well below the $400,000 to $499,000 price point considered the “sweet spot” for a one bedroom Brisbane apartment,” he said.

Allure is within 200m to Cannon Hill’s Kmart Plaza and 150 metres from the forthcoming East Village retail precinct.

## YSH Property Leads the Market | Because Leadership Is More Than Just Numbers

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/ysh-property-leads-the-market-because-leadership-is-more-than-just-numbers/](https://ysh.com.au/ysh-property-leads-the-market-because-leadership-is-more-than-just-numbers/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_10/)

By [priscilla](https://ysh.com.au/author/priscilla/), July 9th, 2025. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

![Property checklist](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2025/07/Ofertas-Relampago-Notebook-Post-Feed-para-Instagam-Preto-Amarelo-e-Branco-1600-x-900-px-2-scaled.png)

In a fast-paced industry where trust, timing and experience make all the difference, **YSH Property** is proud to share a major milestone:

Between **1 January and 3 July 2025**, we were officially ranked **#1 out of 787 agencies on realestate.com.au (REA)**, based on the highest number of leased listings in that period.

This result is more than a metric — it reflects the commitment we bring to every property, every client, and every opportunity.

### A Clear Vision, Delivered with Expertise

What sets YSH apart is a powerful and rare combination:

We bring together **property development, property investment education, and property management** under one roof — creating a holistic and seamless experience for our clients.

At the heart of it all is **Dean Parker**, whose career includes over **450 successful property transactions,** and a leadership style built on **progressive thinking, integrity and results**.

Whether you’re a first-time investor, building your portfolio, or looking for the right team to manage your asset — **you’ll find a partner in YSH** who truly understands your goals.

#### So, Why Does Being #1 on REA Matter

Because it’s not about luck — it’s about strategy. Here’s how we consistently deliver results:

- We understand the Brisbane market inside-out
- We act fast, backed by data and experience
- We market your property where it matters most
- We screen tenants thoroughly, not just quickly
- We communicate openly and honestly

This leads to **shorter vacancy periods, higher rental returns, and long-term peace of mind** for landlords.

We Don’t Just Lease Properties. We Build Trust.

Being number one on REA is an honour — but for us, it’s just one step in a bigger mission:

To provide “*satisfaction beyond the keys*” by supporting our clients from day one, through every stage of ownership, investment or transition.

> Anyone can manage a property.
> We manage people’s peace of mind.
>
> – Dean Parker, Director of YSH Property

##### Are You a Landlord Wanting Real Results

If your property manager isn’t meeting expectations — or if you simply want to make sure your investment is in the best possible hands — we’re here to help.

Request your free rental appraisal

Book a quick call with our team

Let’s talk about how we can lease your property smarter, faster, and more effectively.

###### The YSH Difference

We’re not just agents. We’re developers, educators, and long-term partners.

- As developers, we design modern, energy-efficient properties built with longevity in mind

- As educators, we empower new and experienced investors with practical knowledge through workshops and seminars

- As property managers, we don’t disappear after the lease is signed — we’re here for the life of your investment

Whether you’re buying, renting, or growing a portfolio, we’ve built YSH to support you every step of the way.

###### Let’s Raise the Bar Together

We believe property is more than a transaction — it’s a journey. And every journey is better with the right people beside you.

[**Speak to the team today**](https://ysh.com.au/contact/)

Discover why more landlords are choosing YSH.

###### YSH Property – Built for investors. Focused on results. Driven by people

## The Dangers of Modern Building Products

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_8/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_8/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_7/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_9/)

- [View Larger Image![The Dangers of Modern Building Products Elise Parker Cofounder](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/The-Dangers-of-Modern-Building-Products-Elise-Parker-Cofounder.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/The-Dangers-of-Modern-Building-Products-Elise-Parker-Cofounder.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), March 6th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

I am passionate about health and well-being. In 2007 my father Ron was diagnosed with prostate cancer. He was a healthy person, he ate exceptionally well, was not overweight, did not smoke and had the occasional wine with dinner. So cancer was a real shock. As Dad’s health deteriorated, we offered for him to move into our home and I, who at that time was our full time financial controller in business at the time, but made the decision to take a year off to care for my father. During that time, I became extremely passionate about understanding cancer and why Dad had developed the disease.

I read scores of books and hundreds of articles and what I discovered was that so many household products and building materials contain carcinogens. Carcinogens are known to cause cancer. We reviewed everything in our house from cleaning products, to the food we eat (now mostly organic), to the building materials used in the construction of our home.

The sad truth is that most of the building products used in Australia still have cancer causing carcinogens. Paints, glues, carpets, kitchen cabinetry boards, silicones, etc. That “new house smell” is most likely carcinogening products ‘off-gassing’ and slowly poisoning you. Unfortunately, we were not able to save my Dad, so health and wellbeing is now something our team at Your Style Homes takes very seriously.

### Elise Parker

#### Your Style Homes co-founder

![elisecropped](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/elisecropped.jpg)

## Everything Old is New Again in Ascot

Source: [https://ysh.com.au/blog_4/](https://ysh.com.au/blog_4/)

[Previous](https://ysh.com.au/blog_3/) [Next](https://ysh.com.au/blog_5/)

- [View Larger Image![Everything Old is New Again in Ascot](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Everything-Old-is-New-Again-in-Ascot.jpg)](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/Everything-Old-is-New-Again-in-Ascot.jpg "")

By [Your Style Homes](https://ysh.com.au/author/yourstylehomes/), February 6th, 2017. [News Articles](https://ysh.com.au/category/news-articles/)

We recently had the privilege of transforming a dated, 30-year-old apartment building into a series of stunning, light-filled residences in the blue-chip suburb of Ascot.

The complex, originally built in the 1980s, received a new modern façade while inside, the apartments underwent radical overhauls.

“People love them because they’re so unique and interesting, and they provide spacious living, lacking in so many of the new modern high-rise developments. Unfortunately, there are only a few apartment buildings left in Brisbane suitable for conversion. I know, because I’ve spent a lot of time looking for likely candidates,” Mr Parker said.

An additional three apartments were added to the top of the building and directly bene t from ceilings that soar over three metres high.

The new building facade faces the picturesque Lamington Avenue, and contains nine apartments in total, with the façade mimicking that of a new boutique build. A landscaped central nature strip also provides access to all the levels.

The eclectic mix of accommodation at Rise Ascot includes two and three bedroom apartments. Each has a unique floorplan, interiors embody ‘cool and contemporary’ with a distinct urban edge.

Rise Ascot has 6 two-bedrooms (79 – 93sqm) from $399,950 and 3 three- bedroom apartments (111 – 134sqm) from $635,000.

Mr Parker said Rise Ascot was a rare style of accommodation which would hold great appeal for aspirational buyers.

“The development acknowledges the past and yet reflects the future. The dwellings have everything a modern lifestyle demands, including, Roger Seller kitchen and bath wares, Smeg appliances and split system reverse cycle air conditioning.”

Ascot’s residential renaissance over the past 15 years has made it one of the most popular fringe CBD suburbs.

![YSH 90Lamington023](https://ysh.com.au/wp-content/uploads/sites/8/2018/09/YSH_90Lamington023.jpg)

### Description

Located in one of Brisbane’s most expensive suburbs, with a median house value of $2m, this brand new, purpose-built rooming house delivers an unparalleled 7.14% gross yield against a suburb median of just 2.6%. Finished and income-ready, opportunities like this rarely reach the open market, especially in a grade A suburb like Manly, making this one of the few chances to secure one. Literally a 5-minute stroll to Manly’s finest restaurants, the Esplanade and Manly Harbor, this opportunity is extremely rare.

Premium inclusions built in from day one:

- Split system air conditioning in every room

- Kitchens and kitchenettes fitted with quality appliances

- Security screens and roller blinds to all doors and windows

A location tenants don’t want to leave:

- Multiple bus stops just minutes’ walk away

- Manly Train Station only 2 minutes by car or a 7 minute (500m) walk

- Highly sought after lifestyle location, moments from the stunning Manly Marina

- Surrounded by boutique grocery stores, cafes and restaurants, perfectly suited to modern tenant needs

This is a unique chance to secure a genuinely turnkey investment in one of Brisbane’s tightest and most tightly held markets. Contact Noah Campbell at YSH Property on 0417 637 081 to secure this opportunity before it’s gone.

What is a Rooming House and why Brisbane?

Rooming houses are a relatively new but fast-growing rental option in Brisbane, currently making up around 3% of the city’s rental market. Each property typically holds five fully furnished, individually leased micro-apartments, with a bedroom, living area, kitchenette and bathroom in each, and utilities included for tenant convenience. Occupants are usually young professionals or older single tenants.

Completed rooming houses rarely reach the open market. Limited affordable land and consistent cash flow mean most owners hold them long term, which keeps quality stock in short supply.

Upcoming Brisbane City Council legislation is set to tighten the approval and construction requirements for new rooming accommodation, slowing the flow of new stock considerably. As supply tightens and tenant demand holds firm, securing a newly built, income-ready rooming house now means access to a larger and more concentrated tenant pool down the track. The window to enter this market before competition intensifies is closing, and for investors who move early, the outlook is a strong one.

### Key Statistics

### Address

234 Shafston Ave, Kangaroo Point

### Dwellings

21 Apartments, 2 Available

### Price Range

$559,950

### Construction

Complete

### Wellington Towers

**Luxury Living:** This 3 bedroom apartment has received a complete makeover, making it look and feel brand new. While everything in this apartment has been considered and only premium inclusions have been used, its the attention to detail that makes it truly special. Think herring bone timber look tile flooring, Calacutta Gris engineered stone bench tops and an in built wine fridge just to name a few of the additional extras.

**Enviable Lifestyle:** The only thing that can rival the sophistication of this apartment is the location it is in. Kangaroo Point features unmatched river and city views, the beautiful Kangaroo Point cliffs and of course Brisbane’s most recognisable icon, the famous Story Bridge. The only thing that separates Kangaroo Point from the Brisbane CBD is the beautiful Brisbane River. It is just a short walk along the river to the vibrant entertainment hub of Southbank and Just 300m from your doorstep is Mowbray Park and the Mowbray Park City Cat Ferry Terminal.

**Unbeatable Value:** Situated in a boutique complex of only 21 apartments, this property is a solid investment with low body corporate fees and a high rental return. This is a rare opportunity to secure a high end apartment in one of Brisbane’s most sort after addresses but without the expensive price tag.
